# Current structure round

Updated 2026-10-23. **Production paused at the owner's request for merging, after completing N0771 Würzburg Residence. No next building has started.** The full project remains incomplete: **631 ready candidates / 680 authored and imported individual assets / 657 individual geographic placements / one reviewed regional map type / 369 unfinished candidates.** N0765 Villa Romana del Casale remains a six-member collection with **5/6 complete**; its east latrine stays an authored draft with unresolved exterior/roof state. Saint Isaac remains an authored draft with rejected native ground contact. The owner manages Git.

## Completed model — N0771 Würzburg Residence

[Review](../places/u0/u0z/n0771_wurzburg_residence/QA-REVIEW.md). Complete connected palace exterior on its actual 39-vertex parent outline, retaining the open western Ehrenhof and four mapped inner courts. Sandstone facades, the taller garden pavilion, slate roof subdivisions and curved outer roof masses carry the silhouette. Nearby views add six recessed wooden portals, four front columns on a closed apron, two recessed six-column side loggias, arched/oval upper windows, pilasters, dormers and simplified original roof figures/turret. Ordinary photographs and standard PMTiles/Mapzen guided one continuous author/inspect/fix pass.

- [x] Four authored LODs: **763 / 3,761 / 8,788 / 10,080 triangles**. Initial skyline + district **394,764 bytes**; close-up **859,776 bytes**. Six existing shared 256px texture graphs, six nearby draws/one skyline draw, metric UVs, separate vertex streams and equal CPU/GPU geometry accounting.

- [x] Q156316 / OpenStreetMap relation 7074; anchor [9.938894659280777,49.79276698556261], baked native axes and heading 0. Original 201x201/6m terrain, roads, four court grounds, **1,704 resident / 2,307 wider independent buildings** and **1,915.364m² water** retained. Failure keeps all 1,705 originals; unload restores ground/water and releases geometry. Each used texture graph reads once.
- [x] **Approximate native fit:** floor +0.2m, foundation -2.978m and boundary grades -2.798..+0.482m give supported contact, with low-side plinth exposure up to about 3m and modest high-side burial. Historical levels, thresholds, roof/bay inventory, ornament and accessibility remain approximate. Some detail views crop outer edges; flat synthetic contexts partly bury grade-specific bases and arbitrary neighbors can overlap the enlarged palace context. Whole/native views cover the envelope. Physical device/dense-city timings remain pending.
